<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1000" height="659" viewBox="0 0 1000 659" role="img" aria-labelledby="t d">
<title id="t">abram@github terminal card</title>
<desc id="d">My pig plush profile picture, a pink pig in sunglasses, drawn as colored ASCII art next to a short summary: game dev and machine learning, a game jam platformer, CONFI (an offline PDF tool in Rust), and an Arabic text-to-speech model I fine-tuned.</desc>
